## Run 269 — Shared Character Sheet / Chronicle offline inspection and reconnect recovery — 2026-11-20T15:47Z

**Worker:** Worker B / Build NGL MMORPG. **Runtime PR:** [#713](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/713), draft/unmerged, child of [#712](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/712). Exact base `a4d2e0b9a9f682b2abbac9008dfafbeb2bdbbb2c`; exact code/test head `dff447df2278a48fff99ff077ab425448ad09992`. Documentation checkpoint branch: `docs/run269-offline-chronicle-20261120`.

### Player-facing improvement

The shared Character Sheet now explicitly labels the last accepted shared-world snapshot as stale when the EventSource is disconnected or the browser is offline. The notice includes the accepted world revision, explains that local inspection remains available, and states that shared actions require reconnection and a server-confirmed response. The player can still follow a retained recent-activity link into the exact World Chronicle event while offline; Chronicle labels the disconnected snapshot as potentially stale.

On reconnection, the Character Sheet notice is removed only after the shared stream and accepted state recover. The PWA cache namespace rotates to `20261120-character-sheet-offline-stale-snapshot-21`; shared APIs and authenticated state remain network-only and are not cached.

Four changed paths: `game/client.mjs`, `game/sw.js`, `scripts/verify-combat-yard-mobile.py`, and `test/game-package.test.mjs`. PR #713 currently has 9 commits, 4 changed files, 213 additions and 9 deletions.

### Test-first and correction history

- Test-only commit `49c8b44926a4eb9c948b4e5269cf0383add5f1a1` added the offline Character Sheet assertion before runtime changes. Full Verify [44763235890](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44763235890) and Genesis [44763236068](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44763236068) passed. Combat Yard mobile [44763236186](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44763236186) failed exactly because the stale notice was absent (expected count 1, actual 0).
- Runtime and offline shell changes followed. An intermediate mobile run [44764045182](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44764045182) then exposed a test-baseline error: Evidence Archive navigation legitimately appended a discovery event, so the Character Sheet's newest-six list no longer equalled the earlier combat-only six IDs. Diagnostic commit `769fa7362f58139ac91e1c2feca82d01fbe1f8fe` exposed the exact additional event. Commit `4e80820760bc368c8af31f6ae6312e29ed010917` corrected the assertion to compare against the six links visible immediately before the outage, not the older pre-navigation list. The retained discovery event was preserved.
- Commit `dff447df2278a48fff99ff077ab425448ad09992` extends the mobile journey through reconnection. It verifies the Character Sheet stale notice clears after the stream and server snapshot recover, the accepted revision/updatedAt/XP/event IDs/creation versions remain unchanged, and a newly opened Chronicle is fresh and read-only.

### Independently observed mobile behavior

At both 320px and 390px the real shared-world Combat Yard journey:

## Run 266 — Character Sheet chronology follows retained world-event order — 2026-11-20T13:31:55Z

**Worker:** Worker B / Build NGL MMORPG. **Runtime PR:** [#708](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/708), draft/unmerged, child of documentation checkpoint [#707](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/707). Base is #707 exact head `2138863a61d62970cf9dcee8800c667a35bcc55d`. Final code/test head: `43d14a46da85b7a2d215c6965575a4dd7598e4bc`. Test-only head: `abae3b43a9213c1bd8b591be0524c5d16a52dbb0`.

### Selected defect and player impact

Combat Yard and World Chronicle now use retained event-array position as sequence authority because strict UTC timestamps are metadata and server clocks can regress. The Character Sheet still sorted the actor-scoped recent activity list by wall-clock timestamp. After a valid Combat Yard clock regression, the retained world history could be correct while the player's **Recent field trail** displayed later-appended moves out of order.

The UI iterates `record.recentActivity` directly, so this projection order is the player's visible chronology. The defect affected history comprehension, not combat outcome or XP.

### Test-first reproduction

The test-only commit `abae3b43a9213c1bd8b591be0524c5d16a52dbb0` added a real Scout first-victory sequence with timestamp offsets 10, 20, 15, 30, 25, 40, 35 seconds. Full Verify [44743885047](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44743885047) reproduced exactly one new regression failure: **1,437 passed / 1 failed**. The failure at `test/game-character-sheet.test.mjs:646` showed timestamp-sorted activity IDs differed from retained append order. Genesis [44743885001](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44743885001) PASS.

### Implementation

- `game/character-sheet.mjs`: after actor/role scoping, duplicate-ID elimination and strict per-event timestamp validation, keep the newest six valid events by retained array position with `.slice(-6).reverse()`. Timestamps remain displayed metadata; they no longer reorder the stream.
- `test/game-character-sheet.test.mjs`: the real six-move first victory asserts newest-first retained order, the exact final retained move first, Combat XP 30, Combatant count 1, and a read-only projection.
- `game/sw.js`: rotate the public offline shell to `20261120-character-sheet-retained-order-18`. `/character-sheet.mjs` was already in the core shell; the rotation ensures installed offline clients receive the changed projection.
- `test/game-package.test.mjs`: update the install namespace, current-cache fixtures for activation and optional-art migration, and source assertion. All four stale namespace references were updated after an intermediate run exposed them.

No event schema, combat rules, rewards, skill rules, quests, authority or canonical world state changed. Invalid timestamps remain excluded. Actor ID + role scoping remains unchanged. Character Sheet and recent activity inspection remain read-only. No model/NPC/UI can manufacture progress, evidence, truth, approval or authority.

## Run 265 — Combat Yard clock-skew hardening — 2026-11-20T12:59:37Z

**Worker:** Worker B / Build NGL MMORPG. **Runtime PR:** [#706](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/706), draft/unmerged, child of [#705](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/705). Exact parent head `d0aa2c3bfe5f714e1bbe8f3a05e4c11515b68a81`; final code/test head `e3778915b58ed20b09d380c8977bd758110c19af`. Test-only head `f906c7d1128de9d025582af8faea1813784c9595`.

### Selected defect and player impact

The Combat Yard retained append-only events, and `world.updatedAt` already behaved as a wall-clock high-water mark, but the rules engine still treated timestamps as sequence authority. A server clock correction could reject a legitimate next move, make replay fail, and prevent both model and stored-history validation from accepting an otherwise valid world. This threatens the G2 persistence/recovery contract and the repeatability of the G4 encounter loop.

### Test-first reproduction

The test-only head added three regressions before runtime changes. Full Verify [44739324945](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44739324945) failed exactly those three new cases: pure transition `COMBAT_TIME_REVERSED`, replay `COMBAT_HISTORY_TIME_REVERSED`, and real model action `INVALID_CHRONOLOGY`. The remaining 1,434 tests passed. Genesis [44739325161](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44739325161) passed.

### Implementation

- `game/combat.mjs`: exports the two Combat Yard event types; replays in retained array order; validates every timestamp individually; permits regressed timestamps for a valid next move; and keeps encounter `updatedAt` at its maximum observed timestamp.
- `game/model.mjs`: permits regressed timestamps only for `start-combat-encounter` and `combat-move` actions/events (as Source Observatory already does for its own append-ordered events); all other action chronology remains gated; world `updatedAt` stays at the high-water mark.
- `game/server.mjs`: applies the same narrow exception to stored-world validation and removes obsolete clock-reversal conflict codes.
- `test/game-combat.test.mjs`: verifies regressed pure transitions, encounter high-water behavior, append-order replay, first-win-only 30 game XP, and rejection of malformed timestamps.
- `test/game-model.test.mjs`: executes a real six-move sequence with timestamps 10, 20, 15, 30, 25, 40, 35; verifies append order, world high-water 40, deterministic victory/30 XP, `validateWorld`, and `validateStoredWorldHistory`. A non-combat discovery at 39 remains rejected.
- `docs/gameplay/COMBAT_YARD_RULES_V1.md`: records the append-order and high-water semantics.

The retained log is not sorted or rewritten. Strict normalized UTC timestamps, unique IDs, actor binding, phase rules, replay constraints and first-win-only XP remain. Only the two Combat Yard action/event types receive the clock-regression exception. No model/NPC/UI can grant authority, evidence, truth, approval or progression. No canonical player world was changed by the tests.

### Exact-head verification

- Full Verify [44739549550](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44739549550): **PASS**. Hosted preflight artifact [14629977923](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44739549550/artifacts/14629977923) reports `ready`, warnings `[]`, failures `[]`; diagnostics [14629648877](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44739549550/artifacts/14629648877). ## Run 260 — Chronicle preserves field-item refusal as time-accurate dissent — 2026-11-20T09:31Z

**Worker:** Worker B / Build NGL MMORPG. **Runtime PR:** [#699](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/699), draft/unmerged, child of [#698](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/698) at exact base `4891d9387aad6d1d632c2eaac39fdc7ea8b3c104`. Current code/test head: `d59e10c6c4ca104367b0d3345b3ed5d8fc84d4ae`.

### Objective and implementation

The reducer already retained and validated `field-item-transfer-declined`, and the Field Kit participant history exposed the refusal to sender and designated recipient. Chronicle lacked an explicit case, so the Evidence Archive rendered the refusal as a generic event. Chronicle now projects a first-class **Field-item transfer declined** record linked to the exact earlier offer, item, designated recipient, and original Forge event. Missing/ambiguous/invalid offer lineage or a non-recipient actor remains visible as **Unvalidated field-item refusal** with a warning.

The summary is time-bounded: the refusal did not transfer ownership; the item remained with its owner at the time. It does not imply that the same actor is still the present owner after later events.

### Test-first and correction history

- Test-first commit `965b1ff00bd8c57b2665be5a3ea912e1be976a07` added an actual Forge → offer → designated-recipient decline fixture.
- Original head `97e3b948f92322811e8bed02001bad964708fd45`: Full Verify [44707384921](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44707384921) reported 1,427 pass / 1 fail. The sole failure was a wording mismatch: implementation said “Ownership remains with the current owner,” while the test expected “ownership remains unchanged.” This was not a reducer/ownership failure. Genesis [44707384877](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44707384877) passed.
- Final exact code/test head `d59e10c6c4ca104367b0d3345b3ed5d8fc84d4ae` uses explicit historical wording. Its regression follows the decline with a later valid offer/acceptance by another actor and proves the old refusal remains historically accurate after ownership changes. The decline itself adds no transfer; XP is unchanged; Chronicle and character projections are read-only; a sender-attributed tampered decline remains unvalidated.

### Changed paths

- `game/chronicle.mjs` — first-class validated/unvalidated refusal projection and time-accurate summary.
- `test/chronicle.test.mjs` — later-owner-change regression, no-XP/no-mutation assertions, tampered-actor negative case.
- `game/sw.js` — offline shell namespace `20261120-field-item-decline-chronicle-15`.
- `test/game-package.test.mjs` — offline cache assertions.

No new canonical event, transfer, XP, combat statistic, authority, approval, truth verdict, or evidence certification is created by Chronicle. The existing reducer remains authoritative for validated event IDs; Chronicle is a read-only projection.

## Run 254 — participant-scoped field-item handoff history — 2026-11-20T05:09:38Z

**Worker:** Worker B / Build NGL MMORPG. **Runtime PR:** [#696](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/696), draft/unmerged, child of [#694](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/694) exact base head `99f57fe7c22502e0ed364b3b9841d048a9d93991`. Runtime/test head: `183aeddff08f0bb120eb0521a73b92bd4f592174`; test-first head: `554ac8c9f30ceb56dc58f8906e06751730c8d275`.

### Objective and selected defect

After a designated recipient declined a field-item offer, the incoming-offer card correctly disappeared and ownership correctly remained with the sender. The sender could inspect the Declined result on the current-owner item card, but the recipient could no longer inspect the refusal they authored from their own Field Kit. Chronicle retained the event, but the participant's normal in-world route to their own custody history disappeared.

### Test-first reproduction

The test-only commit added assertions for both participants, unrelated-actor exclusion, and bounded actor history:

- Full Verify [44677275783](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44677275783): **FAIL as intended**, 1,424 passed / 2 failed. The missing projection produced the expected failures: actor history total expected 16 for the bounded-history case and 1 for the declined participant case.
- Genesis Evidence Invariant [44677275873](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44677275873): **PASS**.

### Implementation

- `game/character-sheet.mjs`: derives actor-scoped participant history from reducer-validated offers and outcomes. Sender and designated recipient are matched by both actor ID and role. Each item keeps a bounded recent participant index and full participant count; the projection merges candidates by event position, returns the newest 12 in append order, and discloses the total and omitted count. It includes a minimal item summary, not unrelated item history.
- `game/client.mjs`: adds **MY HANDOFF HISTORY** to the Character Record / Field Kit. Accepted, cancelled, declined and pending outcomes use viewer-relative labels (e.g. “You declined” vs “Recipient declined”) and retain offer/outcome event IDs, timestamps, source reference, and current in-game owner.
- `test/game-character-sheet.test.mjs`: requires both sender and recipient to see the same declined event, an unrelated session to receive no entries, bounded history to retain the newest 12 with correct omitted count, and projection to remain read-only.
- `scripts/verify-quest-board.py`: the 320px/390px journey now inspects the recipient's own declined offer and earlier accepted offer after the incoming card closes; it compares the shared-world snapshot and XP before/after inspection.
- `game/sw.js` and `test/game-package.test.mjs`: rotate and assert offline shell namespace `20261120-field-item-participant-handoff-history-8`.

No new canonical event was added. The projection does not change ownership, inventory, XP, skills, quests, truth, approval, evidence certification, or authority. Session actor IDs remain pseudonymous and do not establish distinct human participants.

## Run 253 — recipient-authored field-item refusal — 2026-11-20T04:36Z

**Worker:** Worker B / Build NGL MMORPG. **Runtime PR:** [#694](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/694), draft/unmerged, child of [#693](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/693) exact head `f56d7f1e747cab826aa74bf4f2da5f38cca7570a`. Current code/test head: `99f57fe7c22502e0ed364b3b9841d048a9d93991`.

### Objective and selected defect

The shared Forge supported sender cancellation and recipient acceptance, but a recipient had no explicit refusal action. A pending offer could therefore remain unresolved until its sender cancelled it. That erased a meaningful player choice: refusal was not distinguishable from silence or sender withdrawal.

The selected improvement adds an event-backed recipient decline, adapting the bounded consent pattern already present in Alpha-1's Guild leadership-transfer action while keeping field-item ownership and Guild authority separate.

### Test-first reproduction

Test-only commit `dcf178f6d8d9b9fbf3de10bac3bb7f6ce29de692` added reducer, character projection, and server integration regressions before runtime changes.

- Full Verify [44674106234](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44674106234): **FAIL as intended** with 1,423 passed / 3 new failures. The projection rejected the unimplemented action, the reducer left the offer `offered`, and the HTTP route returned 400 before reaching the intended recipient-authorization 409.
- Genesis Evidence Invariant [44674106460](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44674106460): **PASS**.

### Implementation

- `game/model.mjs`: added `decline-field-item-transfer`. It requires the shared authoritative world and finds the active offer only in the designated recipient's actor-scoped projection. Sender and unrelated actors receive `FIELD_ITEM_TRANSFER_NOT_ADDRESSED`.
- `game/model.mjs` and `game/server.mjs`: added the `field-item-transfer-declined` event to canonical field allowlists, stored-event detail schema, and idempotency result validation. Replay binds the exact `offerId`; it does not create another event.
- `game/field-items.mjs`: the reducer accepts a decline only for the current active offer, by its exact recipient, after the offer and current item activity. It sets status to `declined`, retains the decline event, and clears the active-offer lock. The item remains owned by the sender; a later offer can be created explicitly.
- `game/character-sheet.mjs`: per-item handoff history retains the decline event ID/time and exposes a `Declined` outcome to the current owner. Pending-offer projections remain pending-only.
- `game/client.mjs`: the recipient's incoming-offer card now has an explicit Decline transfer control. The action is server-confirmed; the UI does not optimistically transfer or mutate the item.
- `game/sw.js` and `test/game-package.test.mjs`: rotated the offline shell namespace to `20261120-field-item-recipient-decline-7`.
- Tests cover recipient-only authorization, sender/wrong-actor rejection, event chronology, re-offer after decline, no ownership/XP/authority change, idempotent replay, and persistence after server restart.
- `scripts/verify-quest-board.py`: the 320px/390px journey exercises the actual Decline button and inspects the sender's retained history read-only.

### Exact-head hosted verification

## Run 250 — actionable Armor Bearer objective selection — 2026-11-20T01:22Z

### Objective and reproduced defect

The actor-scoped Armor Bearer Quest Board selected the first incomplete chain after sorting by progress. An actor could have a 3/4 handoff waiting for another player's equipment while owning a second item with an immediately actionable 1/4 objective. The 3/4 pending chain won selection, but its next action was null because the actor no longer owned that item; the actionable second item was hidden.

Test-first commit `903a52b40d089ef54f31547768a82d9a717a97dc` reproduced this on PR #690 before the runtime fix. Full Verify [44652779041](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44652779041) reported **1,419 passed, 1 failed**; the new regression failed on the three stale evidence IDs returned instead of the second item's Forge event. ## Run 236 — Source Analyst NPC card is Talk-gated — observed through 2026-11-19T14:52Z — Worker B

**Proposed branch only.** Draft code/test PR [#666](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/666), branch `fix/source-analyst-talk-only-20261119-run236`, exact head `a866618f86c77645ef98a2a58de1c8069fb58bf0`. It is a two-commit child of PR #665 exact head `2f968e8efda7d5560796392aa08eefdf937e6e4a`, itself a child of #595 head `38130e230a8a8434cc252dac653b7eb71459907f`. PR #666 remains draft, open and unmerged.

### Selected improvement and implementation

The Source Analyst is a fixed-local NPC stationed at Moonshot Field. Before this change, `openMoonshotField()` appended its NPC workbench card whenever the institution sheet opened, even when the player had not approached or talked to the NPC. That bypassed the world interaction grammar and made a bounded NPC workbench appear as ambient location UI.

Changed paths:
- `game/client.mjs`: append the Source Analyst card only when the caller explicitly opens Moonshot Field with the exact `focusLocalNpcRoleId === 'source-analyst'` NPC interaction.
- `scripts/verify-quest-board.py`: after the normal Quest Board objective directly enters Moonshot Field, assert that `#source-analyst-npc` is absent before Talk; record `sourceAnalystHiddenUntilTalk` in the machine-readable acceptance result. The existing predicate and contrast objectives still route through the Source Analyst's approach/Talk controls and assert that Talk opens and focuses the exact workbench.

Commits:
- `cf8207884a19ca0d74ab2b60b069c7a9161c0b13` — conditional NPC-card rendering.
- `a866618f86c77645ef98a2a58de1c8069fb58bf0` — direct-entry negative acceptance and receipt field.

This is a world-interaction affordance correction, not a new authority or progression mechanism. **Only the NPC identity/guidance card is Talk-gated**; Moonshot Field's general published-source predicate form, retained byte-predicate history, proposals, reports, challenges and responses remain part of the institution and are still accessible on direct location entry. Approach/Talk remain read-only. The NPC makes no model/provider call, retrieval, predicate submission, adjudication, XP award, canonical write, or authority grant. Only the player's separate, explicit predicate submission after public-retention acknowledgement can append the existing server-rechecked byte-predicate event. The source truth remains `NOT_ASSESSED`, independent verification `NOT_PERFORMED`, and model assertions `LEADS_ONLY`.

### Implemented in PR #595

The mobile Quest Board acceptance no longer shortcuts directly into Evidence Archive for the Clerk journey. It now uses the actual accessible world-NPC control:

1. From Guild Hall, focus the exact `[data-guild-npc-control="contradiction-clerk"]` control.
2. First explicit action is `approach`; the workbench remains closed while the player navigates.
3. Arrival changes the control to `talk`.
4. Only the second explicit Talk action opens Evidence Archive and focuses `#contradiction-clerk-npc`.
5. Navigation is allowed to retain only actor-attributed discovery events; observed zero discovery events, zero XP, and no report.
6. Talk is read-only: no actor event, report, XP, or world-revision change.
7. The existing separate explicit challenge submission then appends exactly one actor-attributed report; no XP, truth verdict, approval, or external provider request is produced.

- **Selected gap and rationale:** the proposed Source Archive chain already records an actor-owned proposal and same-version experiment attempt, and the general research system already retains challenges and author responses. However, no dedicated Source Archive quest required a different actor to challenge that exact archive-linked attempt or the original author to answer every linked challenge. This left the research object short of a complete, repeatable adversarial loop. The selected work advances G4 (quests/player progression) and G6 (repeatable research/dissent content) on a proposed branch only.
- **Implementation:** `game/quests.mjs` now projects Source Archive experiment reports across actors only when each report is uniquely linked to its own actor's proposal and the exact same published Source Archive creation/version. Each player receives a challenge objective targeting every other actor's eligible report at least once; multiple challenges against one report count once for that challenge objective, and a newly retained report reopens it. A separate response objective counts only uniquely linked responses by the original report author and requires a response to every valid challenge against that actor's Source Archive report. Wrong-author, duplicate, or ambiguous responses do not advance progress; retained ambiguity blocks retry. Quest evidence details expose challenge type/text and author response text. Interpretations explicitly say challenges/replies are attributed dissent/speech, not adjudication or independent verification.
- **Regression coverage:** `test/game-quests.test.mjs` adds four tests for cross-actor challenge and original-author response, exact same-version linkage, wrong-version exclusion, wrong-author response rejection, multiple challenges against one report, response-per-challenge progression, repeatable target reopening when a new report appears, zero XP, and read-only projection. No XP, truth verdict, independent verification, approval, or authority is created. Failed/inconclusive experiment reports and dissent remain in event history.

- PR #370 branch: game/world-render-lifecycle-mainstack-20261105, draft child of PR #367 exact head dbb49fa.

### Runtime behavior delivered
- The world renderer starts only when a document exists, is visible, the entry doorway is hidden, and no modal sheet is open.
- Mutation observation watches the doorway hidden attribute and body modal-open class; visibilitychange handles background/foreground transitions. Resume occurs only when all gates are clear.
- Pausing clears held keys/touch input, cancels the current route, stops movement, and invokes the existing position callback once when activity was in progress. This is a position-preservation attempt through the existing callback, not a canonical world event or proof that a shared presence request succeeded.
- A monotonically increasing animation epoch prevents callbacks queued before pause from clearing or duplicating the new frame after resume. Destroy is idempotent, cancels the current frame, disconnects the lifecycle observer, and removes the visibility listener.
- No canonical world event, evidence, authority, approval, quest progress, skill, XP, inventory, or world revision is created or changed by this lifecycle guard.

### Selection and change

The highest-leverage remaining gap on the current candidate was not another note form: it was proof that the existing in-world Source Observation action survives both sides of unreliable connectivity without manufacturing canonical state.

This run changed only scripts/verify-browser.py on PR #320. The executable browser journey now verifies two distinct failure modes:

1. **Failure before server receipt:** the browser request is aborted before it reaches the service. The exact local Relay receipt survives; an independent state read observes no Source Observation event, no revision increase, and no XP. The Witness retries the exact actor/action/request ID through The Relay; the server then retains exactly one event and one revision.
2. **Commit followed by lost response:** a second, distinct note is committed by the service, but the browser receives a simulated 503. An independent API state read observes the second event and revision before the Relay retry. Retrying the exact receipt leaves two total events and the same revision—no third event or extra progression.

The same journey checks actor/role and retained-version binding, stable observation/request identity, fixed NOT_PERFORMED / LEADS_ONLY / NOT_EXECUTED assurance fields, unchanged XP, actor-scoped quest completion, Source Recorder projection, Chronicle/Evidence Archive visibility, second-session visibility, and text-only rendering of user content.

The runtime feature already present in PR #320 remains a direct in-world form on each retained Observatory version, using the #317 server-validated append-only event, existing Relay receipt, and deterministic projections. Recording a note is attributed research activity, not source retrieval, independent verification, a truth verdict, approval, XP, or authority.

**Defect reproduction**
- `validateWorld()` accepted a retained `field-item-unequipped` event after its earlier `field-item-equipped` event had been removed, provided revision and timestamp were adjusted. It also lacked active-state checks for duplicate equip/unequip history.
- Test-first commit `aa1006ed5b8ed1cae461284bc14b3790db310515`; Verify [42064044012](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/42064044012) reproduced the defect with the expected missing-rejection assertion. This failure is retained.

**Implementation**
- `game/model.mjs` now replays equipment transitions in append order with a map keyed by actor ID, role and slot.
- Equip must reference an earlier forged item owned by that actor in the matching slot. Selecting a different item replaces the slot selection; selecting the same currently equipped item is rejected. Re-equipping after a valid unequip remains allowed.
- Unequip is accepted only when that actor/role/slot has an active earlier selection. Orphaned, foreign-actor and repeated unequip histories are rejected during world validation.
- `test/game-model.test.mjs` covers orphaned unequip, foreign-actor unequip, repeated unequip and duplicate active equip. Validation creates no item, XP, combat statistic, truth, mastery, approval or authority.

### Defect and implementation
- Defect: Guild Dissent Circuit membership eligibility used timestamps alone. A guild-joined event appended after an observation but carrying a regressed/backdated timestamp could make pre-membership work count in the current roster projection. This conflicted with append-only event order as the causal sequence.
- Fix: game/quests.mjs now indexes the unique current guild-created/guild-joined event position for each current actor. Observations, challenges and responses must be after that membership event in append-only position as well as pass the strict timestamp check. Ambiguous/missing membership events fail closed. Quest inspection remains read-only and awards no XP.
- Regression: test/game-quests.test.mjs constructs a Witness observation before the join event while backdating the join timestamp to precede the observation. The observation is excluded; the circuit remains at 1/4 with only the Founder observation ID. The test protects event order rather than assuming wall-clock timestamps are monotonic.
- Changed paths: game/quests.mjs and test/game-quests.test.mjs. Commit faa1d4b6388417a44b873c4b6fc610d86a6dc272. No canonical world event or XP was created.

### Exact-head verification
- Verify 42128864767 PASS. Node 24 test command, Python inspector, standalone game build and bounded institutional exercise all exited 0. Hosted portable preflight reported zero warnings and zero failures. Artifact 13462217645, SHA-256 e25d71bce3ab0d2df5d494f21da252c1d2de06d0e8c1800713fc7fe5ff17b57c.
- Genesis Evidence Invariant 42128864804 PASS.

The 390px two-context journey created/joined a guild, retained two linked observations, one later challenge and one author response; both clients reloaded at 4/4, shared world converged, no quest XP was awarded, board inspection was read-only, and page errors were empty. Both observations remained NOT_PERFORMED for retrieval/independent verification and LEADS_ONLY for model assertions. Artifact 13463025162, SHA-256 49ebebce7614ab2778c4babd6fcd2a33ecdd402c1355ece08fdd8b0f7b7444d6.
- Browser Acceptance 42128864782, Synthesis Clerk mobile 42128864765 and Android Alpha-1 42128864797 were still in progress at the last status read; none is counted as a pass. Parent PR #371 Android run 42114619901 passed 7/7 emulator instrumentation tests on its own exact head; this does not establish physical-device acceptance or shared multiplayer in the offline APK.

### Observed failure and challenge
- PR #375 exact-head workflows: Verify [42129430104](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/42129430104), Genesis [42129429944](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/42129429944), Browser Acceptance [42129430121](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/42129430121), Quest Board mobile [42129429961](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/42129429961), and Synthesis Clerk mobile [42129430751](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/42129430751) passed. Android Alpha-1 [42129430501](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/42129430501) failed one of seven instrumentation tests after APK assembly and portable preflight succeeded.
- Failure: `nativeWorldExportAndImportRoundTripsEarnedProgress` timed out at `MainActivityTest.java:547` while waiting for the reset private world after it cleared local storage and called `location.reload()`. The job log confirms the WebView URL, page title, progress 100 and attached state, but the combined wait did not identify which of its four DOM predicates was false.
- Source challenge: the post-reload wait checked `document.readyState`, the `start-private` handler and `window.__nglNativeFileBridgeInstalled`. All three can already be true in the old document, so they do not prove that Chromium committed a new document before the test clicked. This is a plausible acceptance race, not a proven application-state defect. The passing parent #371 emulator run [42114619901](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/42114619901) is not evidence for the newer #375 stack.

### Bounded correction
- `android/app/src/androidTest/java/labs/nogas/commonground/MainActivityTest.java` now marks the old document with a one-shot `window.__nglAndroidReloadMarker`, requests reload, and waits for that marker to disappear alongside the new document's ready state and entry handler before clicking. The new-world check now waits for play UI separately and asserts private mode, zero creations and zero XP as distinct assertions.
- `test/android-workflow.test.mjs` now statically requires the fresh-document marker/wait and the separate empty-world assertion, preventing a return to the stale-document readiness check.
- Changed paths: those two test files only. No game/runtime source, event schema, canonical world, XP, inventory, authority, permission, credential, release or deployment state changed. The correction tests the Android WebView reload boundary; it does not add an Android multiplayer service or claim physical-device acceptance.

The next action is to create/inspect the draft child PR, then inspect the new hosted preflight, Verify, and Android job logs. ## Run 79 — 2026-11-05 — On-demand MMORPG world rendering

### Baseline and rehydration
- Current main remains `6f1a4aefb87334ddad359af12f754505965e29a5`; the current game/Android stack remains proposed and unintegrated. PR #376 is the fresh-document Android acceptance child of #375/#371. PR #378 is a draft child of #376, not a mainline change.
- Re-read current-main root AGENTS.md, the complete shared scheduler protocol, and the current-main institutional charter; re-read branch root instructions and branch protocol. The PR #376/#378 branch predates the current institutional charter and does not contain `docs/institution/INSTITUTIONAL-CHARTER.md`; the current-main charter remains governing and must be reconciled by the integration owner before merge.
- Read the latest checkpoint and completion ledger. Checked nested AGENTS.md under game/, test/, docs/, docs/operations/, android/, android/app/, android/app/src/androidTest/ and .github/; none were present. No connected execution device or local checkout was available, so local portable preflight was not run. Exact-head hosted Verify ran the declared portable preflight.
- Rechecked PRs #366, #367, #370, #371, #375, #376 and other-worker PRs #373/#374. The existing #370 lifecycle guard pauses behind the entry doorway, modal sheets and hidden documents, but the current WorldView frame callback still continuously rescheduled while idle. PR #309 remains the recovered lifecycle donor; this change extends the active WorldView path rather than copying a separate renderer.

### Implementation
- Draft PR #378: [perf(game): sleep the world renderer between changes](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/378), branch `game/idle-render-scheduling-verified-20261105`, code/test head `8150b12d5c8eda0bdcd727472c29220adfd63ee8`, base PR #376 branch `game/android-reload-acceptance-race-20261105` at `5f75a5539717962d007ca75e65cd9a54a2b0713a`. Eight commits ahead of that base, zero behind.
- `game/world-view.mjs`: the renderer now draws on invalidation and only continues requestAnimationFrame while player movement, a route, or camera easing remains active. The camera target calculation is shared by update and the animation predicate. When the renderer sleeps, its prior frame timestamp is cleared; a later touch/key action starts with a fresh 1/60-second initial delta rather than applying idle time to movement.
- State changes that wake a frame include world projection, presence, resize, touch input, route creation/cancellation, blur/resume, and camera movement. `game/pixel-world-view.mjs` also wakes after quest-target changes, map-overlay mutation (without layout reads inside the MutationObserver), and the NPC-route override.
- Tests in `test/game-geometry.test.mjs` and `test/pixel-world-view.test.mjs` cover one-shot idle rendering, world/presence/resize invalidation, continued movement, camera convergence, idle-time delta reset, quest/overlay invalidation, and NPC-route wake without an interaction or canonical event.
- This is renderer scheduling only. No canonical event, evidence, quest progress, inventory, XP, truth, approval, consensus, or authority is created. No measured CPU/battery reduction or physical-device result is claimed.

### Failure found and corrected
- The first draft PR #377 was closed as superseded by #378; it was not merged. On its initial code/test head `559afba6cd8573d89cb037a9a148a48745a0ba94`, Synthesis Clerk mobile workflow [42151172579](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/42151172579) timed out waiting for the Synthesis NPC control to become `talk`.
- The failing journey exposed a real invalidation omission: `PixelWorldView.goTo()` overrides the base route method and writes `this.route` directly, so the new on-demand renderer never received a wake request for NPC navigation. PR #378 adds the wake after the NPC route is set and a regression test proves navigation schedules a frame but does not interact or create canonical state.
- The same first draft had Verify, Genesis, Quest Board and Academy pass; the Synthesis failure was preserved rather than hidden. The superseding PR includes the NPC-route correction and its test.

## Run 95 — 2026-11-06 — Worker B — Server-checked shared movement and discovery recovery

### Bootstrap and repository baseline

- Read current-main `AGENTS.md`, the complete `RALPH_SHARED_HOURLY_SCHEDULER_PROTOCOL.md`, Institutional Charter, Universal Agent Operating Contract, bootstrap checkpoint, hourly-agent registry, stutter-step steering, forensic steering and do-not-delete instructions before further edits.
- Checked for nested `AGENTS.md` under the touched `game/`, `test/`, `scripts/`, `.github/` and `docs/operations/` paths; none were present. Root instructions govern these flat paths.
- Local repository checkout was not available and the registered Desktop Commander devices were offline. Therefore no local `npm run agent:preflight` or local Node 24 execution is claimed. Hosted Verify on code/test head 6a3959 ran the repository preflight and reported `ready`, zero warnings and zero failures; receipt artifact 13534499633 is preserved.
- Current main remained `6f1a4aefb87334ddad359af12f754505965e29a5`. PR #383 is the current-main snapshot parent; PR #384 is the child feature branch. No branch was merged.

### Implementation and corrections

The active PR #384 began this run with a server-checked movement implementation. Run 95 continued it and repaired the failures exposed by exact-head browser/mobile verification:

1. `game/world-view.mjs`: `WorldView.setWorld` rebuilds its local discovered-guide set from retained discovery records belonging to the active actor ID. Another actor does not inherit that discovery.
2. `game/client.mjs`: the active viewer ID is passed into the world projection. The reconnect handler now completes pending Relay action replay before fetching/reconciling session position, avoiding a stale state response racing ahead of a recovered mutation.
3. `game/pixel-world-view.mjs`: the subclass now forwards the viewer ID to `WorldView.setWorld`. The Browser run on intermediate head 62e923 exposed that the override had silently dropped the third argument; the resulting empty local set emitted repeated Relay discovery events. The subclass regression now covers this exact forwarding boundary.
4. `game/pixel-world-view.mjs`: accessible Guild NPC controls are bounded and placed above the bottom toolbar, with scrolling on short screens and `pointer-events:none` while visually hidden. This repairs the Places-button interception observed on an earlier Synthesis Clerk mobile run.
5. `test/game-geometry.test.mjs` and `test/pixel-world-view.test.mjs`: cover actor-scoped discovery rehydration in the base view and actual pixel renderer, plus the mobile NPC-control hit area and hidden pointer behavior.
6. `scripts/verify-browser.py`: separates travel from NPC interaction. Travel may append only unique actor-local guide discoveries; the subsequent NPC talk must leave events, revision and XP unchanged.
7. `scripts/verify-synthesis-clerk.py`: accepts the case where the server-restored player is already within NPC interaction range and the accessible route opens Guild Hall without a redundant canvas tap.
8. The server movement feature in this PR uses a monotonic server clock, walkable-route validation and a bounded movement-speed envelope for per-session positions. The `discover` action is accepted only when the server-held session position is within the guide radius. Position updates do not themselves append world events or grant XP.

### Exact code/test-head verification — 6a3959dfeef03b8ef6c25b79d2dccf40d7cedf1f

- Verify run 42281653461: PASS. Hosted preflight `ready`, warnings 0, failures 0; Node 24 repository tests exit 0; Python inspector exit 0; standalone game build exit 0; institutional exercise passed. Receipt artifact 13534499633.
- Genesis Evidence Invariant run 42281653508: PASS.
- Browser Acceptance run 42281653781: PASS. Artifact 13536161118 contains browser receipts/screenshots for desktop, mobile, shared-world and recovery journeys.
- Synthesis Clerk mobile run 42281653592: PASS. Artifact 13535103665 preserved.
- Council Chamber mobile run 42281653848: PASS.
- Academy Study Shelf run 42281653822: PASS.
- Quest Board mobile run 42281653618: IN PROGRESS at the Playwright/Chromium installation step when this checkpoint was written; no result claimed.
- Android Alpha-1 run 42281653720: QUEUED when this checkpoint was written; no result claimed.
- Earlier failed runs remain part of the history: head fbdf5a exposed re-entry assumptions; head 26918 exposed a redundant NPC canvas tap; head 62e923 exposed the PixelWorldView actor-ID forwarding defect. Each was attributed from its exact workflow log and repaired on the branch. No failure was deleted or re-labelled as a pass.

### What the evidence establishes — and does not establish

- Server tests exercise direct discovery denial without a nearby accepted position, rejection of too-fast movement, rejection of an invalid/water path, bounded walking to a guide, per-session position isolation and exact Relay replay. The browser suite confirms actor-local guide discovery and that NPC talk does not create a canonical event or XP.
- Rehydrated discovery is a deterministic client projection of retained actor-attributed discovery records. It is not proof of truth, external evidence, physical presence, a distinct human or Founder authority.
- Position is currently held in server session memory, not persisted across server process restart. A restart returns the session to spawn; no persistent-character-position claim is made.
- Actor IDs remain pseudonymous. No GPS, physical-device location proof or distinct-human authentication was added.
- The Android package remains offline-only and has no INTERNET permission. Emulator evidence on parent PR #383 is not transferred to PR #384's queued Android run.
- Forge item transfer remains unimplemented. The existing Forge donor supports creation/equip/unequip, not offer/cancel/accept ownership transfer.
- Founder trust anchor remains `NOT_ENROLLED`. Current-main `game/server.mjs` contains no live `authorizationEvent` call or decision route; the helper test's temporary `TEST_ONLY` key is not production authority. No key or authority transition occurred.

### Next action

Finish or recheck the pending Quest Board and Android workflows on the exact code/test head, then verify the documentation-head checks. After current-main snapshot review/integration and governance reconciliation, the next high-leverage persistence task is to store the last server-accepted actor/session position across process restart with atomic recovery and two-session isolation. Only then resume the separate Forge offer/cancel/accept transfer loop. Keep G3/G6/G7 IN PROGRESS, G8 NOT_ENROLLED, and G9 NOT STARTED until their respective evidence exists.

## Run 98 — 2026-11-06 — Worker B — Forge two-session ownership transfer

### Bootstrap and repository baseline

- Read current-main `AGENTS.md`, the complete shared scheduler protocol, Institutional Charter, Universal Agent Operating Contract, bootstrap checkpoint, hourly-agent registry, stutter-step steering, forensic steering and do-not-delete instructions before editing. The root governance files on the #385 branch matched current main; no nested `AGENTS.md` was present under touched `game/`, `test/` or `scripts/` paths.
- Re-inspected current main `6f1a4aefb87334ddad359af12f754505965e29a5`, PR #366, the current-main snapshot PR #383, movement/position child PR #384, Forge donors #367/#379 and the recovered player-owned field-inventory donor. PR #366 remains 1 ahead / 2 behind main at merge base `950a976560354ffa86ddd3b01f9c6d42d4247d22` and still lacks current-main root/protocol/Charter reconciliation.
- Recovered branch `game/forge-transfer-20261106-run98` had no PR. Opened draft PR #385 as a child of #384. Final code/test head `a33d178dcea56c95a813e69d6daf6b33c47eb6fa` is 22 commits ahead / 0 behind #384 and 51 ahead / 0 behind main. Main was not changed.

### Implementation and repairs

1. `game/model.mjs`: retained `field-item-transfer-offered`, `field-item-transfer-cancelled`, and `field-item-transferred` events. Validation replays offer state, current owner, designated recipient, equipped-slot state and inventory capacity. Only the designated recipient can accept; only the current owner can cancel. A pending offer blocks equip/unequip changes; equipped items cannot be offered. Exact Relay request receipts remain actor/action-bound.
2. `game/character-sheet.mjs`: deterministic current-owner inventory projection replays accepted transfer events while preserving original creator, forge event and source event. Creator history remains visible to Chronicle after an item leaves the creator's inventory. Transfer event IDs are exposed to the read-only Chronicle projection.
3. `game/server.mjs`: offer creation requires the designated actor to have a recent live shared event stream, not merely a recently touched session cookie. Opening an SSE stream now registers presence at the server-held position even if the player has not moved; disconnect removes presence when the final stream closes.
4. `game/client.mjs`: Forge field-kit UI exposes recipient selection, incoming recipient-only acceptance, owner cancellation and current-owner/creator/source labels. The UI explicitly states that transfers grant no XP, combat statistics or authority.
5. `game/quests.mjs`: transfer quest progress is derived from retained accepted transfer events for the participating actors. It is an objective projection, not a model/NPC declaration, and grants no XP.
6. `game/chronicle.mjs`: offer, cancellation and accepted ownership changes have explicit entries. The original forge event remains valid history after transfer; a malformed/tampered acceptance remains visible with a warning rather than being projected as ownership.
7. Tests: model coverage includes owner/recipient restrictions, equipped-item and pending-offer guards, capacity, concurrent acceptance and exact replay; server coverage includes inactive-session rejection, live presence, restart and two-session ownership; quest coverage checks event-derived progress and zero XP; Chronicle coverage checks offer/cancel/transfer entries and tampered acceptance; browser journey exercises the full 320px two-session flow, cancellation, acceptance, inventory movement, immutable creator provenance, unchanged XP and no page errors.

The first exact-head Verify attempts exposed two attributable defects and they were retained in the branch history: the model validation map stored an offer without its `offerId`, so cancellation/acceptance compared the active offer to undefined; and the new Chronicle test looked for `eventId` although Chronicle's stable key is `id: world:<eventId>`. Both were repaired before the final code/test head.

### Attributable Browser Acceptance failure and repair

- The first Browser Acceptance on docs head `e71b5533b88a89f387e022f6e0dd12f86893acbb`, run [42306180808](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/42306180808), failed at `scripts/verify-browser.py` line 2124. Playwright's default `locator.wait_for()` waited for an `<option>` to become visible, but option elements are intentionally hidden children of a visible `<select>`. The option was present; the test assertion used the wrong visibility state. The failure was in the browser harness, not the game runtime.
- Repaired the locator to use `wait_for(state="attached")` and committed [`10c4c745c0c0851723997be083c0ddb401323da6`](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/commit/10c4c745c0c0851723997be083c0ddb401323da6), commit message `test(browser): wait for recipient option attachment`.

### Selected gap

The Commons already limited each actor to five posts per minute and allowed only one report by an actor against a particular message. The `report-message` action had no per-actor limit across different messages. One pseudonymous session could therefore submit an unbounded burst of distinct reports and flood the human moderation queue. This was a bounded, unowned G5 abuse-control gap; the existing Founder moderation and append-only report mechanisms were reused rather than replaced.

### Implementation — draft PR #386

- PR #386: [fix(game): rate-limit Commons reports per actor](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/386), branch `game/commons-report-rate-limit-20261106-run11`, draft child of PR #385.
- Code/test head: `8298510412577f0ad34ce4270fe3c338f10cafa1`, eight commits ahead / zero behind base `c70d940ff16bd862163de565af15931e61efc702`; 66 commits ahead / zero behind current main. PR metadata reported mergeable, open, draft and not merged.
- `game/social.mjs`: adds the shared five-reports-per-actor-per-minute limit using the existing 60-second Commons window.
- `game/model.mjs`: applies the admission check before appending a new `message-reported` event; the sixth distinct report within the window returns `COMMONS_REPORT_RATE_LIMITED` / HTTP 429. Future-dated retained events are not counted as already-submitted reports.
- Exact request-ID replay remains resolved by the server before new action admission. A lost-response retry therefore recovers its original report receipt instead of consuming another report slot.
- Historical reports remain readable. The new admission rule does not retroactively invalidate or discard older/imported report history; it governs new report actions only.
- `test/game-social.test.mjs`: covers five accepted reports, sixth-report rejection, duplicate-report precedence, window expiry, and preservation of an over-limit historical record.
- `test/game-server.test.mjs`: creates six messages from two authors; verifies five accepted reports, exact request replay, a sixth HTTP 429, and unchanged revision/report count/XP after rejection.
- `test/agent-governance.test.mjs`: explicitly keeps `game/social.mjs` and `test/game-social.test.mjs` in the hosted preflight target regression.
- No XP, truth, evidence, approval, moderation authority, Founder authority, or world progress is awarded by reporting or rate rejection.

### Selected defect and implementation
- The active PR #391 branch was already network-first for navigation but still cache-first for non-navigation shell assets. The first fix made assets network-first, but the response path still awaited Cache Storage before attempting the network. A slow Cache Storage open could therefore block a successful online response.
- A second iteration moved the cache write into a background promise, but inspection showed the original response could be consumed before the cache was opened and cloned. That would preserve the online response but could lose the offline cache update.
- Final code/test head: \`d6e37d5acb42632b8591696a6f5250b1d94a7c64\`, branch \`fix/pwa-network-first-shell-20261106-run11\`, draft PR #391, child of PR #390 head \`65b2e9371f5a1e93592a5383c0bc9383d49d2acd\`, 8 commits ahead / 0 behind that base.
- \`game/sw.js\` now starts network requests immediately for navigation and allowlisted shell assets. \`scheduleCacheResponse\` registers a cache-update promise through \`event.waitUntil\` during event dispatch. \`cacheResponse\` clones a successful response before awaiting Cache Storage, so the client receives the network response without waiting for cache open/write. Cache updates continue in the background.
- On transport failure or HTTP 5xx, the last cached response remains the fallback. HTTP 4xx stays visible. The allowlist and stable cache namespace are unchanged. Same-origin, no-query, allowlisted public shell paths only are cached; \`/api\`, authenticated/private data, query URLs and cross-origin requests remain excluded.
- Verified adjacent integration: \`game/server.mjs\` serves static assets with \`Cache-Control: no-cache\`; \`game/client.mjs\` registers \`./sw.js\`.
- \`test/game-package.test.mjs\` now has 21 top-level test cases. Two new slow-Cache-Storage regressions cover both an online shell asset and navigation, asserting the online response returns and the offline copy is cloned before Cache Storage is awaited. Existing stale-refresh, offline, 503, 404, missing-fallback, cache-failure, navigation and API-exclusion tests remain.
- No canonical event, world revision, inventory, XP, quest progress, evidence, authority, identity or server persistence was changed.

### Bootstrap and baseline

This establishes the parent head, not the new PR #392 head.

### Selected gap and test-first reproduction
- PR #391 already made navigation and allowlisted shell assets network-first and moved cache updates into `event.waitUntil`, but fetch-time `openCache()`, `matchCache()`, and `cache.put()` only handled rejection. A never-settling Cache Storage promise could still leave offline navigation/asset fallback pending indefinitely or keep the fetch event's background lifetime pending indefinitely.
- Test-only commit `afbc9d6e98b73546d48a87ef08911dc4be08662b` added five deadline-bounded regressions. Verify run 42337628523 reproduced all five failures: 994/999 tests passed; the five new tests each exceeded the 1,800 ms response deadline. Genesis 42337628541 passed. Browser run 42337628614 was cancelled after the test-only head was superseded; it is not counted as a pass.
- The failure was a reproduced missing bound in the existing fetch-time cache path, not a canonical-world or gameplay failure.

### Implementation
- Draft PR #392, branch `fix/pwa-bounded-offline-cache-20261106-run106`, child of PR #391, code/test head `87b6ce97ee60323f565157578afcfddc57e8f881`.
- `game/sw.js` adds a 1,000 ms per-operation bound for fetch-time Cache Storage open, match and put. Rejection or timeout yields no cache result; online network responses remain available, offline fallback fails visibly instead of hanging, and a 503 remains visible if cached fallback lookup cannot finish.
- `test/game-package.test.mjs` injects timers into the service-worker harness and adds five regressions: stalled cache open for offline asset/navigation; stalled cache match for offline asset; stalled cache match while handling HTTP 503; and stalled background put while preserving the online response and allowing the `waitUntil` promise to settle.
- The existing public-shell allowlist, stable cache namespace and network-only API/private-state boundary are unchanged. No whole RAE/Mesh/Relay/RuneUX/Guild/Command Center/Decision Chamber/TownSquare/AGP donor was copied; this closes a gap in the existing service-worker mechanism.
- The timeout bounds how long this worker waits; Cache Storage exposes no AbortSignal, so an already-started operation may still finish later. This is a per-operation bound, not one total navigation deadline. Install/activate cache lifecycle calls (`addAll`, `keys`, `delete`) are not changed by this PR.

### Selected defect
- Commons already treats a session actor as `(id, role)` for report rate limits and duplicate-report admission, and Guild uses the same composite identity. But `publicWorld`, TownSquare report visibility, self-report checks, and retained packet reconstruction had ID-only comparisons.
- A same-ID/different-role actor could receive a private report in the public world projection, have another role's report misclassified as its own, or be incorrectly prevented from reporting a message authored by the other role.
- A second consistency defect affected ordinary distinct sessions: a Witness packet omitted another actor's private `message-reported` event, while server history validation counted that hidden event. The packet snapshot could therefore be rejected at durable commit even though packet creation and the model validator agreed.

### Implementation
- Draft PR #393, branch `game/commons-report-actor-scope-20261106-run107`, child of PR #392 exact head `669dbc180fe9422acd00388913b41f57257c0670`.
- Code/test head `87def4bc232a326e47d88a710ae6128429b6f2f9`; compare is 8 commits ahead / 0 behind base, 8 changed files.
- `game/model.mjs`: report self-checks, `publicWorld` privacy filtering, retained-history validation and collaborator packet event counts now compare both actor ID and role.
- `game/social.mjs`: TownSquare private-report visibility and `reportedByMe` use the existing composite Commons actor key.
- `game/collaborator-response.mjs`: retained Council response reconstruction uses the same role-aware report filter.
- `game/server.mjs`: durable history validation counts the same Witness-visible event prefix as packet construction and `validateWorld`.
- Tests in `test/game-social.test.mjs`, `test/game-model.test.mjs`, `test/game-collaborator-response.test.mjs`, and `test/game-server.test.mjs` cover same-ID/different-role privacy, cross-role self-report semantics, retained Council packet reconstruction, model snapshot validation, and a two-Witness/Founder HTTP flow where a Witness persists an advisory response while another actor's private report remains excluded.
- The server regression verifies that the retained packet does not contain the private report details, the Founder can still inspect the report, and the collaborator action leaves XP at 0. No NGL authority or moderation decision is granted.

### Implementation and added regression
- Draft PR #398: [repeatable Guild Dissent Circuit](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/398), branch `game/repeatable-guild-dissent-circuit-20261107-run11`, based on PR #397.
- `game/quests.mjs` preserves the first objective ID and projects later rounds as `<role>-guild-dissent-circuit-2`, `-3`, and so on. - Changes on #423:
  - `game/quests.mjs` now projects one milestone per `COMBAT_OPPONENTS` entry. The existing Scout objective ID is preserved. Warden is actionable only after the same actor ID+role has a retained first victory against Scout; Champion is actionable only after that actor's retained Warden first victory.
  - Each completion requires exactly one matching retained `combat-move-resolved` event for the projected first-win record. Missing/ambiguous matches or malformed combat history withhold the entire Combat Yard ladder. Locked objectives have no action and explain the prior-opponent prerequisite.

The registered Desktop Commander device was unavailable, so no local checkout, local portable preflight, or local Node test run is claimed. - Baseline: draft PR #457 head `e58af2b834723b4ef658fbc2adc326d783d33ef2`, child of #456 head `5d6d629fda9b44c4dce4be5637613097599ba623`. On #457, Verify `42812516923`, Genesis `42812516932`, Academy `42812517020`, Council `42812516933`, Quest Board `42812517053`, and Synthesis Clerk mobile `42812516950` passed. Browser Acceptance `42812516953` failed in `combat_yard_shared` at the post-arrival position-radius assertion. The assertion did not report actor coordinates. Parent #456 Browser Acceptance `42808064156` passed the same journey. Android Alpha-1 `42812516883` was still in progress at this checkpoint. Worker A PR #454 head `04746850c3b774fd3d21ffb76d38d57dab612de5` had Verify `42773895867`, Genesis `42773896100`, and Browser `42773896056` passing; its process-local authority provenance change does not overlap game position paths.
- Source diagnosis: `WorldView.update` emits accepted-position callbacks periodically while moving and again when movement stops. The client previously issued each `/api/presence` fetch directly, allowing more than one write to be in flight. The server validates and commits positions in request-arrival order using its walkable-path and movement-speed envelope. Concurrent requests can therefore arrive out of local emission order; an older coordinate can be processed after a newer one. This is a real race class in the implementation. It is a plausible cause of the #457 failure, not a proven explanation of the exact failed coordinates.
- Implementation in draft child PR #458: code commit `d6487e19ddeca683fa2e3aef886747783ea71c85`, parent #457 exact head above. `game/player-position.mjs` adds a FIFO transport writer that reuses the existing own-data, finite, in-bounds coordinate validator. `game/client.mjs` serializes shared position writes, invalidates older response corrections, drops queued stale coordinates after a failed write, coalesces state reconciliation, and uses the reconciled renderer position for the next write. `test/game-player-position.test.mjs` covers FIFO/no overlap, failed-write queue invalidation, subsequent recovery write, and getter-safe validation. `scripts/verify-browser.py` now includes actor ID, role, coordinates, target and distance in the Combat Yard assertion.
- These are auxiliary session-position transport changes only. They create no world event, XP, inventory, quest progress, evidence, approval, NPC authority, or institutional state. Position remains separate from the canonical world event log.
- Exact-head #458 workflows were triggered for code commit `d6487e19ddeca683fa2e3aef886747783ea71c85`: Verify `42814890372`, Genesis `42814890167`, Browser `42814890146`, Android `42814890149`, Council `42814890337`, Quest Board `42814890384`, Synthesis Clerk mobile `42814890297`, Academy `42814890394`. These results apply only to the baseline head, not the new change.
- New proposed runtime change adds a server-issued session `positionEpoch` and monotonic `positionRevision`. `/api/presence` is compare-and-set: stale epochs/revisions receive HTTP 409; successful revisions advance only after the existing atomic session-position persistence succeeds; server-side relocation also advances the revision. The client ignores stale same-epoch position snapshots, bounds each shared position request to 10 seconds, aborts the transport on deadline, and fails closed if reconciliation cannot read current server state. The existing one-in-flight/latest-pending queue remains bounded.
- The timeout is explicitly an uncertain transport outcome, not proof that the server did not commit. The server-issued epoch/revision fences a late request that reaches the queue after a newer coordinate. Movement remains session state only: no world event, XP, quest progress, evidence, approval, inventory or authority is created.

### Implementation
- `game/world-view.mjs`: `projectSourceArchiveFailureObjects` now groups valid retained failure records by the same request/action binding using the existing digests internally. Each record receives a deterministic failure-class position/count and a conservative completeness flag. The request digest is not exposed in the player projection. If the world snapshot is partial or any retained failure record is invalid, the sequence is marked incomplete.
- `game/client.mjs`: Evidence Archive failure cards show “Distinct retained failure class N of M for this same request/action” and expose bounded data attributes for acceptance. Partial or invalid history is explicitly not presented as a complete sequence.
- `game/sw.js`: rotated the offline shell to `20261119-source-archive-failure-class-sequence-1`; package tests assert the namespace and sequence disclosure.
- `test/game-world-institution-activity.test.mjs`: covers separate request groups, two distinct failure classes for one request/action, partial history, invalid history, and no mutation.
- `test/game-server.test.mjs`: covers the actual HTTP retry path: first a temporary upstream-unavailable result, then a byte-receipt mismatch after transport recovery, then an exact repeated mismatch. The two distinct failure classes remain as two separate retained events; the repeated mismatch is deduplicated. XP remains zero and no draft is created.
- `test/game-package.test.mjs`: verifies the player-facing sequence metadata and truth-boundary disclosure.

### Semantics preserved
- The server's existing model is attempt-oriented: different service-observed failure classes for the same request/action are distinct retained outcomes; repeating the same failure class for that binding is deduplicated. The request digest remains bound to one action. This preserves failure history rather than collapsing different observed failures into one.
- A test-first hypothesis that all failure classes for a request/action should collapse to one was rejected after inspecting the server route and persisted-history validator. The exploratory test-only commit `71cbd22343ccc12dec3eba1ec7f611d335c43555` produced two expected test failures because the existing semantics deliberately retain distinct failure classes. The final tests now assert the actual contract. No server event semantics were changed.

## Run 249 — ownership-period-bound Armor Bearer progression — 2026-11-20T00:25Z

- **Runtime PR:** [#688](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/688), draft/unmerged, child of [#686](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/pull/686). Branch `game/armor-bearer-ownership-epoch-20261119-run249`; base #686 exact head `40572e8b810e80fb8dced480221de934febfab95`; current exact code/test head `77b445c11bbe208f5ec70c41106549540081733a`.
- **Defect:** the original projection reused any equip after Forge. When a source-bound item was handed off, returned to its prior bearer, and offered onward without a new equip, the earlier ownership-period equip could satisfy the later handoff. The old projection also failed to expose a new current-ownership objective after the item returned while an earlier chain remained complete.
- **Test-first evidence:** test-only commit `e21ffb02cf147db1d4258706aa3c7a70a00e558c`; Full Verify [44644867851](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44644867851) ran 1,419 tests and failed one new assertion at `test/game-field-items.test.mjs:342`: the returned item had no new current-ownership chain. Genesis [44644867902](https://github.com/ngl-damien/No-Gas-Labs-Alpha-1/actions/runs/44644867902) PASS. The same regression includes stale-equip rejection and fresh-re-equip acceptance assertions; the red run stopped at the first missing-chain assertion, so do not claim the later stale-equip assertion independently failed on that red run.
- **Runtime correction:** `game/field-items.mjs` now derives each actor's latest accepted transfer-in position as the start of the current ownership period. A bearer equip counts only when it follows that acquisition and precedes the outgoing offer. An accepted transfer after a stale equip remains in history but contributes only the valid Forge prefix (1/4) and is visibly blocked; a later recipient equip cannot complete it. A returned item opens a new current-owner chain even when an earlier handoff remains complete. A fresh equip after reacquisition allows the next handoff to complete.
- **Quest regression:** `test/game-field-items.test.mjs` covers returned ownership, stale-equip rejection, fresh re-equip acceptance and read-only projection. `test/game-quests.test.mjs` executes actual Forge/equip/offer/accept/return actions and proves the returned item becomes the current 1/4 Quest Board objective, inventory reflects the return, XP is unchanged, and projection does not mutate the world.
- **Offline mobile regression:** `test/game-package.test.mjs` now asserts `field-items.mjs` is in the offline core shell because `quests.mjs` imports it. `game/sw.js` already lists `/field-items.mjs`; this PR adds a guard rather than changing the shell or cache namespace.
- **Changed paths:** `game/field-items.mjs`, `test/game-field-items.test.mjs`, `test/game-quests.test.mjs`, `test/game-package.test.mjs` (four files).
